# Phase 1 progress

This is the record of the Phase 1 build in `phase-1-plan.md` section 6, newest first.
Each entry gives the date and time in UTC, the step, the commits, the test count, the budget, the golden ids moved, what was proven and any deviation from the plan.

## Log

  - Interpretation: the window is in UTC: January 1 of the year ten back to yesterday's UTC date, and the daily means' latest day lags a day or two, so `stats.to` comes from the days the answer holds, not from the window. A day with no value is left out, and a negative one (USGS marks a missing reading -999999) too; a day listed twice counts once.
  - Interpretation: only alerts may answer with nothing. Any other slot that answers 200 with an empty list, no keys, or only empty lists and nulls fails.   - Critique, of plan 6 (P1.17), 08 10.11 and the code, written before any change. (1) The block. The Content-Security-Policy line in the plan, in 08 10.11 and in `public/_headers` is the same 644 characters (compared by script). Read against what the app does: the built `index.html` and `privacy.html` have one external module script and no inline script, no `on...` attribute and no frame; the CSS has three `url()` fonts and all are files of the same origin (no `data:` font); `src/` has no worker, blob, eval or `new Function`; the service worker is registered by `virtual:pwa-register` from `src/main.js` (`worker-src 'self'`); the Turnstile script is added by `src/suggest-form.js` (`script-src` and `frame-src`); the town search is `placesUrl()` in `src/picker.js`; `/api/*` is `connect-src 'self'`. Inline `style` attributes need `'unsafe-inline'` in `style-src`, and the browser spec shows it: without it 5 of 5 checks fail. `geolocation=(self)` is what Near me needs. (2) Item 2 is already true: all ten adapters in `src/sources/` export `hosts` and all have `via: 'browser'`, so `src/sources/` is unchanged. (3) A gap in the plan's tests. `hosts` is written by hand on each adapter, so "every host of every adapter is in `connect-src`" passes if an adapter names the wrong host. The URL it builds is what the browser asks. Added: for each of the seven records under `data/`, the origin of every URL `bindingsFor()` builds (53 URLs, 5 hosts) is in its adapter's `hosts` and in `connect-src`. A host the app names that no adapter owns (the town search, Turnstile) is covered by a sweep of the https hosts written in `src/`, `src/copy/` left out (those are links, not requests): each must be in `connect-src`, `script-src` or `frame-src`. (4) A gap in the plan's check. "By hand, because `vite preview` does not apply `_headers`" means nothing guards the policy after this step: a later screen that asks for a new host, or writes an inline script, would break production and pass CI. Added a lasting browser spec that gives every response of the preview server the file's `/*` headers.   - Heads-up, what the count is. A page load is a start: the one reload a new service worker causes after a deploy is a second `return` for that visitor, and a browser that restores a tab at startup counts too. An offline start is not counted, since the request never arrives. Anyone can send the three words, so the count is an aggregate and not tamper proof. The plan has no rate limit on `/api/info`.

(3) A source never fetched has no time, and `!(now - t <= max)` reads it as past its limit, as the NaN rule the P1.8 gate asked for says.   - Gate, defect found and fixed (619cc6a). The page for no location drew a second search box. `openPicker()` awaits the list of locations, and `show()` draws the page again on each visit to an address: tap Try again on a location that did not load while the first search is still waiting for the list (a slow or flaky connection, which is when a location fails to load), or go Back between two addresses with no location. Both draws then answer after the second `page()` call has replaced the panel, and both append their search to it: two search boxes, and the older one could be the one with the older address in it. Found with a scratch spec that held `locations/index.json` (2 boxes, 2 index requests). Fix: `openPicker()` counts the draws of the page (`drawn`, not the sheet's) and a draw that finds a newer one started returns without drawing: a counter and one condition in the lazy chunk, nothing in the main script (main JS still 29,586 B).   - Critique, before the change, of the plan's step against the code at 2c86314, against the loops, the guards and the sizes. (1) `parseRoute` is read by `p-config` through the probe, so the probe moves with `parseHash` in the same commit as the new function (the plan's commit (b)); it does, and `p-config` does not move. (2) `src/state.js` resolved Putney while it loaded, and four tests read `app.loc` at import (`test/storage.test.js` three times, `test/format.test.js`, `test/suggest-context.test.js`, all of `test/settings.test.js` through `applyOverrides`). They change with the code, since none can run against code that has no `openLocation()`. (3) A double event: Chromium fires `popstate` and then `hashchange` for a move to another tab, so `show()` on both would draw a view twice per tab click, where it was once. The router shows on `popstate` only when the path or the query changed, and on every `hashchange`. When a path and a hash both change, both events show, so `open()` has to be safe to call twice for one address. (4) A slow record for one address must not open after another was asked for. My first guard, "the slug asked for last", did not cover going to A, then to B (slow), then back to A: A is open already, so nothing was asked and B opened late over it. A counter does (`asked` in `src/main.js`: every `open()` takes the next number, and an answer for an older number is dropped), and it also stops a second call for the address in flight from leaving two openings. (5) The `hidden` attribute loses to `.tabs{display:flex}` and `.actions{display:flex}`, so the plan's "tab bar and buttons hidden" needs a CSS rule and an id on `.actions`. (6) `validateSlug` in the main script brought 276 B of `src/location.js` with it (the error text, `bad()`, `RESERVED_SLUGS`): see Deviation.
  - Changed, `src/state.js`: nothing resolves at import (`app.loc` is null until a location opens). `app` gains `record`, `token` (counts the locations opened) and `refreshing` (the token of the refresh in flight). `openLocation(record, overrides)` sets `app.record`, the loc, the settings (with the viewer's changes in force) and the defaults (the location's limits, and its heading only for a heading course, never the viewer's), resets `data`, `errors`, `updated`, `fromCache`, `model`, `openPeriods`, `detailDay` and `homeScroll`, and adds 1 to `token`. A record of `null` leaves no location open and counts as an opening, so a refresh in flight is dropped. `applyOverrides(o)` resolves again from `app.record`. `takeRefresh(token, { data, errors })` returns false and changes nothing for an old token; otherwise it keeps the last good copy of a failed source, names the failures, and dates a good forecast. `canRefresh()` is true with a location open and no refresh in flight for its token.
  - Changed, `src/locations.js` (new): `cachedRecord(slug)` (`{ record, fetchedAt }` when the saved record has `v` 1 and this slug, else null), `fetchIndex()` (judged by its body, kept once read, a failure not kept), `fetchRecord(slug)` (`{ record }` when the body parses as `v` 1 and this slug whatever the content type or status; else the index decides: `{ missing: true }` when it lacks the slug, `{ failed: true }` when it lists it, or when it cannot be read, or on a network error) and `newerRecord({ record, fetchedAt })` (fetches again when the index lists another revision, or the copy is over 24 hours old; saves what it fetched, which dates the copy again; gives it back only when its revision is not the saved one's). A record with no revision is 1.
  - Changed, `src/route.js`: `parseRoute({ pathname, hash, search })` gives `{ page, slug, view, day, open }` (and `path` for a not found address); `parseHash(hash, keys)` is the old route with the keys passed in; `homeTarget({ last })` gives `{ slug, replace: true }` for a stored slug that passes `isSlug`, else `{ slug: 'putney-landing', keepAddress: true }`; `navigate(href)`, `navClick(e)`, `renderTabs()`, `showTab()`, `showMissing(path)`, `showFailed()`, `setDay(day)` and `initRoutes(openSlug)`. `show()` reads the address, replaces `/` with `/<slug>` and the hash when a last location is stored, and calls the opener, which main.js supplies. The not found page and "Could not load this location" hide the tab bar, `#home`, `#detail`, Refresh and Settings and show `#notFound`; the second has Try again.
  - Changed, `src/main.js`: `refresh()` does nothing without a location or while a refresh for the same token is in flight, keeps its token, clears `app.refreshing` in a `finally` only if it is still its own, keeps the Refresh button off while another location's refresh runs, and after `saveCache()` calls `trimCaches()`. `enter(record, keepAddress)` opens the location, `touchCache`, `saveLast` unless `keepAddress`, `pushRecent`, draws the tabs, the Settings reminder and the tab, draws the saved forecast whatever its age (R1), then refreshes. `open(slug, keepAddress)` takes the record from this browser, else from `fetchRecord()`, drops an answer that is no longer the latest asked for, shows the not found page or the failed page, and after the first draw replaces a changed record (`newerRecord`) when the same location is still open. Nothing in `src/` imports `src/putney.js` any more.

  - Critique, before the change, of the first builder's code and tests, against the plan's list, the guards, the loops and the tests. Against the list it matches: the four keys and two constants, every function the plan names with its signature, `saveCache` retrying at most 3 times by the cap's rule, `migrateStorage()` comparing with a frozen `OLD_PUTNEY_DEFAULTS` in `src/config.js`, no import of `src/putney.js` left in `src/storage.js`, and no `localStorage` call outside a try block. Four findings. (1) Both loops ended only if storage behaved: `trimCaches` looped while the index was over 10, and `saveCache` counted down in a loop with no limit in its header. A store that ignored the index write, or one removed `return`, would spin on every page load. Mutations that did this hung the test run. Changed: each loop is bounded by construction (see Deviation). (2) The outer guard of `saveCache` had no test: a throw while making room, or data that `JSON.stringify` cannot write, passed with the guard deleted (mutation S20 survived). Three tests added. (3) For P1.4, `loadLast()` returns any string, so `homeTarget()` must check it with `validateSlug` before it builds an address or a request from it.   - Changed, the harness (`test/golden/harness.js`): the write mode is an update mode. `GOLDEN_UPDATE=1` rewrites the file of a case whose comparison fails, or writes a missing one, for the cases that run, then fails the run on purpose with the test "is not in update mode". The rewrite goes through `sameOrder()`, so the old file's key order stays and new keys follow. It is refused when `CI` is set: the cases compare as usual, nothing is written, and the same test fails with the refusal. `GOLDEN_UPDATE_LOG` names a file that gets each case written. `GOLDEN_DUMP` takes one name or several joined by commas.
